*Janavi Gupta,Kavya Puthuveetil,Dimitra Tsakona,Akhil Padmanabha,Yiannis Demiris,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"隐式控制"交互范式，通过用户自然行为线索而非直接输入来修改自主机器人的行为，旨在降低感知工作负荷并保持用户控制感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着辅助机器人系统在改善残疾人生活质量方面潜力日益增长，研究者在探索各种护理任务自动化的同时，如何保持用户控制感成为确保机器人系统符合用户基本需求和动机的关键考虑因素。

Method: 通过两个先前开发的系统作为设计案例，探索隐式控制交互范式，并对两个案例的用户感知进行定性反馈的主题分析。

Result: 研究发现，有效的隐式控制设计能够通过系统的直观性和响应性、上下文感知能力以及偏好适应能力，既降低感知工作负荷，又保持用户的控制感。

Conclusion: 研究进一步为设计者制定了一套核心指南，用于决定何时以及如何将隐式交互范式应用于辅助应用。

Abstract: Assistive robotic systems have shown growing potential to improve the quality of life of those with disabilities. As researchers explore the automation of various caregiving tasks, considerations for how the technology can still preserve the user's sense of control become paramount to ensuring that robotic systems are aligned with fundamental user needs and motivations. In this work, we present two previously developed systems as design cases through which to explore an interaction paradigm that we call implicit control, where the behavior of an autonomous robot is modified based on users' natural behavioral cues, instead of some direct input. Our selected design cases, unlike systems in past work, specifically probe users' perception of the interaction. We find, from a new thematic analysis of qualitative feedback on both cases, that designing for effective implicit control enables both a reduction in perceived workload and the preservation of the users' sense of control through the system's intuitiveness and responsiveness, contextual awareness, and ability to adapt to preferences. We further derive a set of core guidelines for designers in deciding when and how to apply implicit interaction paradigms for their assistive applications.

</details>


### [116] [$AutoDrive\text{-}P^3$: Unified Chain of Perception-Prediction-Planning Thought via Reinforcement Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28116)
*Yuqi Ye,Zijian Zhang,Junhong Lin,Shangkun Sun,Changhao Peng,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoDrive-P³是一个将感知、预测和规划通过结构化推理无缝集成的自动驾驶框架，解决了现有VLM方法中缺乏协同推理和模块分离的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动驾驶方法存在两个主要局限：1）一些VLM直接输出规划结果，绕过关键的感知和预测阶段，造成领域差距并损害决策能力；2）其他VLM虽然能生成感知、预测和规划输出，但采用分离的决策方法，各模块独立运行，缺乏协同性，影响真正的规划性能。

Method: 提出AutoDrive-P³框架，通过结构化推理无缝集成感知、预测和规划。创建P³-CoT数据集促进连贯推理，并提出P³-GRPO分层强化学习算法，为所有三个任务提供渐进式监督。框架逐步生成感知、预测和规划的链式思维推理和答案，感知为后续预测和规划提供关键信息，感知和预测共同贡献于最终规划决策。还引入双重思维模式（详细思考和快速思考）以平衡推理效率与性能。

Result: 在开环（nuScenes）和闭环（NAVSIMv1/v2）基准测试上的广泛实验表明，该方法在规划任务中实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: AutoDrive-P³通过结构化推理将感知、预测和规划无缝集成，解决了现有VLM方法的局限性，实现了更安全、更可解释的自动驾驶，并在规划任务中达到了最先进的性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being adopted for end-to-end autonomous driving systems due to their exceptional performance in handling long-tail scenarios. However, current VLM-based approaches suffer from two major limitations: 1) Some VLMs directly output planning results without chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, bypassing crucial perception and prediction stages which creates a significant domain gap and compromises decision-making capability; 2) Other VLMs can generate outputs for perception, prediction, and planning tasks but employ a fragmented decision-making approach where these modules operate separately, leading to a significant lack of synergy that undermines true planning performance. To address these limitations, we propose ${AutoDrive\text{-}P^3}$, a novel framework that seamlessly integrates $\textbf{P}$erception, $\textbf{P}$rediction, and $\textbf{P}$lanning through structured reasoning. We introduce the ${P^3\text{-}CoT}$ dataset to facilitate coherent reasoning and propose ${P^3\text{-}GRPO}$, a hierarchical reinforcement learning algorithm that provides progressive supervision across all three tasks. Specifically, ${AutoDrive\text{-}P^3}$ progressively generates CoT reasoning and answers for perception, prediction, and planning, where perception provides essential information for subsequent prediction and planning, while both perception and prediction collectively contribute to the final planning decisions, enabling safer and more interpretable autonomous driving. Additionally, to balance inference efficiency with performance, we introduce dual thinking modes: detailed thinking and fast thinking. Extensive experiments on both open-loop (nuScenes) and closed-loop (NAVSIMv1/v2) benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in planning tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/haha-yuki-haha/AutoDrive-P3.

</details>


### [117] [A Position Statement on Endovascular Models and Effectiveness Metrics for Mechanical Thrombectomy Navigation, on behalf of the Stakeholder Taskforce for AI-assisted Robotic Thrombectomy (START)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28129)
*Harry Robertshaw,Anna Barnes,Phil Blakelock,Raphael Blanc,Robert Crossley,Rebecca Fahrig,Ameer E. Hassan,Benjamin Jackson,Lennart Karstensen,Neelam Kaur,Markus Kowarschik,Jeremy Lynch,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich,Dwight Meglan,Vitor Mendes Pereira,Mouloud Ourak,Matteo Pantano,S. M. Hadi Sadati,Alice Taylor-Gee,Tom Vercauteren,Phil White,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C. Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 建立AI辅助机器人取栓术的共识框架，包括标准化有效性指标和定义跨计算模拟、体外、离体、体内环境的参考测试平台


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卒中发病率上升，大血管闭塞治疗时间窗短且受地理限制，机器人手术系统结合AI辅助可能改善偏远地区及时取栓的可及性

Method: 召集神经介入、机器人、数据科学、卫生经济学等多领域专家，通过孵化日、德尔菲法和最终立场声明建立共识框架

Result: 确定了四种测试环境各自独特的验证作用：计算模拟、体外、离体、体内；定义了技术导航和临床结果两类宏观有效性指标；强调了患者安全的核心地位

Conclusion: 需要建立AI辅助机器人取栓术的标准化验证框架，特别关注患者安全，当前急需将体外测量与体内并发症关联起来

Abstract: While we are making progress in overcoming infectious diseases and cancer; one of the major medical challenges of the mid-21st century will be the rising prevalence of stroke. Large vessels occlusions are especially debilitating, yet effective treatment (needed within hours to achieve best outcomes) remains limited due to geography. One solution for improving timely access to mechanical thrombectomy in geographically diverse populations is the deployment of robotic surgical systems. Artificial intelligence (AI) assistance may enable the upskilling of operators in this emerging therapeutic delivery approach. Our aim was to establish consensus frameworks for developing and validating AI-assisted robots for thrombectomy. Objectives included standardizing effectiveness metrics and defining reference testbeds across in silico, in vitro, ex vivo, and in vivo environments. To achieve this, we convened experts in neurointervention, robotics, data science, health economics, policy, statistics, and patient advocacy. Consensus was built through an incubator day, a Delphi process, and a final Position Statement. We identified that the four essential testbed environments each had distinct validation roles. Realism requirements vary: simpler testbeds should include realistic vessel anatomy compatible with guidewire and catheter use, while standard testbeds should incorporate deformable vessels. More advanced testbeds should include blood flow, pulsatility, and disease features. There are two macro-classes of effectiveness metrics: one for in silico, in vitro, and ex vivo stages focusing on technical navigation, and another for in vivo stages, focused on clinical outcomes. Patient safety is central to this technology's development. One requisite patient safety task needed now is to correlate in vitro measurements to in vivo complications.

</details>


### [118] [Reducing Mental Workload through On-Demand Human Assistance for Physical Action Failures in LLM-based Multi-Robot Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28156)
*Shoichi Hasegawa,Akira Taniguchi,Lotfi El Hafi,Gustavo Alfonso Garcia Ricardez,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REPAIR是一个基于LLM的多机器人协调框架，通过人类在环的远程干预解决物理执行失败问题，显著提升了任务进度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的多机器人协调系统在物理执行失败时难以恢复，任务常因重复失败动作而停滞。虽然已有混合现实远程操作框架，但专门针对多机器人环境中物理故障的远程错误解决方案较少

Method: 提出REPAIR框架，将远程错误解决集成到LLM多机器人规划中。机器人自主执行任务，当发生不可恢复故障时，LLM向操作员请求协助，通过远程干预保持任务连续性

Result: 在真实环境的多机器人垃圾收集任务评估中，REPAIR相比完全自主方法显著提升了任务进度（时间限制内清理物品数量）。对于易收集物品，任务进度与完全远程控制相当。结果还表明操作员心理负荷在物理需求和努力方面可能存在差异

Conclusion: REPAIR框架通过人类在环的远程错误解决机制，有效解决了LLM多机器人系统中的物理执行失败问题，在保持任务连续性的同时平衡了自主性和人工干预

Abstract: Multi-robot coordination based on large language models (LLMs) has attracted growing attention, since LLMs enable the direct translation of natural language instructions into robot action plans by decomposing tasks and generating high-level plans. However, recovering from physical execution failures remains difficult, and tasks often stagnate due to the repetition of the same unsuccessful actions. While frameworks for remote robot operation using Mixed Reality were proposed, there have been few attempts to implement remote error resolution specifically for physical failures in multi-robot environments. In this study, we propose REPAIR (Robot Execution with Planned And Interactive Recovery), a human-in-the-loop framework that integrates remote error resolution into LLM-based multi-robot planning. In this method, robots execute tasks autonomously; however, when an irrecoverable failure occurs, the LLM requests assistance from an operator, enabling task continuity through remote intervention. Evaluations using a multi-robot trash collection task in a real-world environment confirmed that REPAIR significantly improves task progress (the number of items cleared within a time limit) compared to fully autonomous methods. Furthermore, for easily collectable items, it achieved task progress equivalent to full remote control. The results also suggested that the mental workload on the operator may differ in terms of physical demand and effort. The project website is https://emergentsystemlabstudent.github.io/REPAIR/.

</details>


### [119] [Off-Axis Compliant RCM Joint with Near-Isotropic Stiffness and Minimal Parasitic Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28240)
*Federico Mariano,Elena De Momi,Giovanni Berselli,Jovana Jovanova,Just L. Herder,Leonardo S. Mattos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于神经内窥镜操作的离轴、整体式柔性远程运动中心关节，结合了近各向同性刚度和最小寄生运动，基于Tetra II概念设计，将末端执行器置于四面体柔性机构外部以改善视线、便于灭菌和快速工具更换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经内窥镜手术需要精确的远程运动中心(RCM)机制来避免脑组织损伤，传统RCM机构存在视线遮挡、灭菌困难和工具更换不便等问题。本文旨在开发一种紧凑、易于制造、具有良好刚度各向同性和最小寄生运动的RCM关节。

Method: 采用两阶段设计方法：首先基于柔度各向同性目标确定移动面板尺寸，然后通过有限元可行性探索合成约束面板，在刚度各向同性和RCM漂移之间进行权衡。使用梁单元建模并通过详细有限元分析验证，包括疲劳约束应力分析。采用选择性激光烧结技术制造PA12原型，并通过6自由度电磁传感器在实验台上进行表征。

Result: 所选配置产生刚度椭圆主轴比(PAR)为1.37，寄生旋转与有用旋转比(PRR)为0.63%。在4.5°指令旋转下，预测的RCM漂移保持在亚毫米级(0.015-0.172 mm)。疲劳分析预测可用旋转工作空间为12.1°-34.4°（取决于方向）。实验再现了模拟的方向刚度趋势，典型偏差为6-30%。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一种紧凑、可制造的RCM模块，具有近各向同性刚度、最小寄生运动和亚毫米级RCM漂移，适用于受限手术通道的神经内窥镜操作，解决了传统RCM机构的视线遮挡、灭菌和工具更换问题。

Abstract: This paper presents an off-axis, monolithic compliant Remote Center of Motion (RCM) joint for neuroendoscopic manipulation, combining near-isotropic stiffness with minimal parasitic motion. Based on the Tetra II concept, the end-effector is placed outside the tetrahedral flexure to improve line of sight, facilitate sterilization, and allow rapid tool release. Design proceeds in two stages: mobility panels are sized with a compliance-based isotropy objective, then constraining panels are synthesized through finite-element feasibility exploration to trade stiffness isotropy against RCM drift. The joint is modeled with beam elements and validated via detailed finite-element analyses, including fatigue-bounded stress constraints. A PA12 prototype is fabricated by selective laser sintering and characterized on a benchtop: a 2 N radial load is applied at the end-effector while a 6-DOF electromagnetic sensor records pose. The selected configuration produces a stiffness-ellipse principal axis ratio (PAR) of 1.37 and a parasitic-to-useful rotation ratio (PRR) of 0.63%. Under a 4.5° commanded rotation, the predicted RCM drift remains sub-millimetric (0.015-0.172 mm). Fatigue analysis predicts a usable rotational workspace of 12.1°-34.4° depending on direction. Experiments reproduce the simulated directional stiffness trend with typical deviations of 6-30%, demonstrating a compact, fabrication-ready RCM module for constrained surgical access.

</details>


### [120] [Cost-Matching Model Predictive Control for Efficient Reinforcement Learning in Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28243)
*Wenqi Cai,Kyriakos G. Vamvoudakis,Sébastien Gros,Anthony Tzes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模型预测控制（MPC）的强化学习框架，通过成本匹配方法优化人形机器人运动控制，避免训练中重复求解MPC问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MPC方法需要手动调整成本函数参数，且训练过程中重复求解MPC问题计算负担大。需要一种能自动学习最优控制策略、同时保持计算效率的方法。

Method: 采用参数化MPC公式（基于质心动力学），通过成本匹配方法训练：将MPC剩余成本沿记录的状态-动作轨迹进行评估，更新参数以最小化MPC预测值与实际回报之间的差异。避免训练中重复求解MPC问题，实现高效的基于梯度的学习。

Result: 在商用仿人机器人平台上进行仿真验证，相比手动调优的基线方法，表现出更好的运动性能和鲁棒性，能够有效应对模型失配和外部扰动。

Conclusion: 成本匹配方法成功地将MPC与强化学习相结合，实现了高效的最优控制策略学习，为人形机器人运动控制提供了一种计算高效且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a cost-matching approach for optimal humanoid locomotion within a Model Predictive Control (MPC)-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) framework. A parameterized MPC formulation with centroidal dynamics is trained to approximate the action-value function obtained from high-fidelity closed-loop data. Specifically, the MPC cost-to-go is evaluated along recorded state-action trajectories, and the parameters are updated to minimize the discrepancy between MPC-predicted values and measured returns. This formulation enables efficient gradient-based learning while avoiding the computational burden of repeatedly solving the MPC problem during training. The proposed method is validated in simulation using a commercial humanoid platform. Results demonstrate improved locomotion performance and robustness to model mismatch and external disturbances compared with manually tuned baselines.

</details>


### [121] [osmAG-Nav: A Hierarchical Semantic Topometric Navigation Stack for Robust Lifelong Indoor Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28271)
*Yongqi Zhang,Jiajie Zhang,Chengqian Li,Fujing Xie,Sören Schwertfeger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: osmAG-Nav：基于OpenStreetMap区域图层次语义拓扑地图标准的开源ROS2导航栈，解决大规模多楼层环境中传统占用栅格地图的存储效率、跨楼层推理和长时程规划瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航栈依赖单体占用栅格地图，在大规模多楼层环境中面临存储效率低、跨楼层推理困难、长时程规划计算量大等严重瓶颈，需要既能实现空间可扩展性又不牺牲局部运动精度的导航系统。

Method: 采用"系统之系统"架构，将全局拓扑推理与局部度量执行解耦。核心包括：1）分层osmAG规划器，在通道中心图上使用LCA锚定管道替代密集栅格搜索；2）滚动窗口机制，在机器人周围栅格化固定大小的局部度量网格；3）分段执行策略，向标准ROS2控制器分派中间目标；4）结构感知LiDAR定位框架，基于永久建筑先验过滤动态干扰。

Result: 在真实世界多楼层室内外校园环境（>11,025 m²）的实验中，在同楼层基准子集上，osmAG-Nav在长路径上的规划延迟比基于栅格的基线低7816倍，同时保持低路径长度开销和终身定位稳定性。单楼层长距离机器人任务进一步验证了集成栈的可靠性。

Conclusion: osmAG-Nav通过层次语义拓扑地图标准解决了大规模多楼层环境中的导航瓶颈，实现了空间可扩展性而不牺牲局部精度。系统采用模块化ROS2生命周期节点发布，为大规模环境中的机器人导航提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: The deployment of mobile robots in large-scale, multi-floor environments demands navigation systems that achieve spatial scalability without compromising local kinematic precision. Traditional navigation stacks, reliant on monolithic occupancy grid maps, face severe bottlenecks in storage efficiency, cross-floor reasoning, and long-horizon planning. To address these limitations, this paper presents osmAG-Nav, a complete, open-source ROS2 navigation stack built upon the hierarchical semantic topometric OpenStreetMap Area Graph (osmAG) map standard. The system follows a "System of Systems" architecture that decouples global topological reasoning from local metric execution. A Hierarchical osmAG planner replaces dense grid searches with an LCA-anchored pipeline on a passage-centric graph whose edge costs derive from local raster traversability rather than Euclidean distance, yielding low-millisecond planning on long campus-scale routes. A Rolling Window mechanism rasterizes a fixed-size local metric grid around the robot, keeping the local costmap memory footprint independent of the total mapped area, while a Segmented Execution strategy dispatches intermediate goals to standard ROS2 controllers for smooth handoffs. System robustness is reinforced by a structure-aware LiDAR localization framework that filters dynamic clutter against permanent architectural priors. Extensive experiments on a real-world multi-story indoor-outdoor campus (>11,025 m^2) show that, on the same-floor benchmark subset, osmAG-Nav delivers up to 7816x lower planning latency than a grid-based baseline on long routes while maintaining low path-length overhead and lifelong localization stability. A single-floor long-range robot mission further validates the integrated stack reliability. The full stack is released as modular ROS2 Lifecycle Nodes.

</details>


### [122] [Point of View: How Perspective Affects Perceived Robot Sociability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28272)
*Subham Agrawal,Aftab Akthar,Nils Dengler,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过VR实验发现，从鸟瞰视角评估为社交友好的机器人轨迹，在近距离第一人称视角下可能被视为显著干扰，而头部点头等社交信号能有效提升感知社交性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人导航验证方法主要依赖鸟瞰视角，无法捕捉行人在真实世界中遭遇机器人时的第一人称主观体验，存在感知视角与真实体验之间的差距。

Method: 使用沉浸式VR环境，在用户研究中评估相同机器人轨迹在三种视角下的感知：鸟瞰视角、第一人称近距视角和第一人称远距视角。分析两种不同导航策略生成的轨迹，并测试头部点头手势是否能弥合感知差距。

Result: 实验表明：1）鸟瞰视角下评估为社交友好的轨迹，在近距离第一人称视角下可能被视为显著干扰；2）通过距离影响感知干扰程度；3）头部点头等社交信号能有效提升机器人行为的感知社交性。

Conclusion: 机器人导航验证需要考虑第一人称视角体验，社交信号（如头部点头）能有效改善人类舒适度，未来机器人导航设计应整合视角感知差异和社交信号。

Abstract: Ensuring that robot navigation is safe and socially acceptable is crucial for comfortable human-robot interaction in shared environments. However, existing validation methods often rely on a bird's-eye (allocentric) perspective, which fails to capture the subjective first-person experience of pedestrians encountering robots in the real world. In this paper, we address the perceptual gap between allocentric validation and egocentric experience by investigating how different perspectives affect the perceived sociability and disturbance of robot trajectories. Our approach uses an immersive VR environment to evaluate identical robot trajectories across allocentric, egocentric-proximal, and egocentric-distal viewpoints in a user study. We perform this analysis for trajectories generated from two different navigation policies to understand if the observed differences are unique to a single type of trajectory or more generalizable. We further examine whether augmenting a trajectory with a head-nod gesture can bridge the perceptual gap and improve human comfort. Our experiments suggest that trajectories rated as sociable from an allocentric view may be perceived as significantly more disturbing when experienced from a first-person perspective in close proximity. Our results also demonstrate that while passing distance affects perceived disturbance, communicative social signaling, such as a head-nod, can effectively enhance the perceived sociability of the robot's behavior.

</details>


### [123] [Proposing a Game Theory Approach to Explore Group Dynamics with Social Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28348)
*Giulia Pusceddu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究社交机器人能否促进群体成员合作，采用公共物品博弈游戏理论方法评估机器人影响力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将社交机器人融入群体社会需要考虑社会群体动态，前期探索性研究表明社交机器人对群体决策有影响，需要进一步研究机器人是否能促进群体合作

Method: 采用博弈论方法，使用公共物品博弈游戏来重现简化和受控的社会情境，评估机器人的影响力

Result: 论文尚未提供具体实验结果，但提出了研究框架和方法论

Conclusion: 明确机器人在促进人类协作方面的作用可能对教育环境（增强学生学习）和工作场所（促进问题解决和共享解决方案）产生重要影响

Abstract: Integrating social robots in our group-based society, beyond the technical challenges, requires considering the social group dynamics. Following the results from preliminary exploratory studies on the influence of social robots on group decisions, the proposed research investigates whether social robots can foster cooperation among group members. To achieve this, I propose a game theory approach, employing the Public Good Game to recreate a simplified and controlled social situation where the robot's influence can be evaluated. Clarifying the role of robots in promoting collaboration among humans might have a significant impact in educational environments, enhancing student learning, as well as in workplace settings, where they could facilitate problem-solving and lead to shared solutions.

</details>


### [124] [A Foldable and Agile Soft Electromagnetic Robot for Multimodal Navigation in Confined and Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28362)
*Zhihao Lv,Xiaoyong Zhang,Mengfan Zhang,Xiaoyu Song,Xingyue Liu,Yide Liu,Shaoxing Qu,Guoyong Mao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种小型可折叠软电磁机器人(M-SEMR)，具有9种以上运动模式，能在胃肠道等复杂环境中实现多模态运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物在非结构化野生环境中需要多模态运动来适应环境，同样在人类胃肠道(具有粘弹性粘液、复杂皱襞和狭窄括约肌)中，小型软机器人也需要多模态运动能力来执行任务

Method: 设计了一种小型紧凑、可折叠、坚固的软电磁机器人，采用六辐条弹性体结构，嵌入液态金属通道，在静磁场下通过拉普拉斯力驱动，实现快速模式切换

Result: 机器人实现了高速滚动(818 mm/s，26 BL/s)、全向爬行、跳跃和游泳等多种运动模式，可折叠减少79%体积，能在离散障碍物、粘弹性明胶表面、粘性流体和模拟生物组织等复杂地形导航

Conclusion: 该系统为开发用于未来生物医学应用的高机动性软机器人提供了一种多功能策略

Abstract: Multimodal locomotion is crucial for an animal's adaptability in unstructured wild environments. Similarly, in the human gastrointestinal tract, characterized by viscoelastic mucus, complex rugae, and narrow sphincters like the cardia, multimodal locomotion is also essential for a small-scale soft robot to conduct tasks. Here, we introduce a small-scale compact, foldable, and robust soft electromagnetic robot (M-SEMR) with more than nine locomotion modes designed for such a scenario. Featuring a six-spoke elastomer body embedded with liquid metal channels and driven by Laplace forces under a static magnetic field, the M-SEMR is capable of rapid transitions (< 0.35 s) among different locomotion modes. It achieves exceptional agility, including high-speed rolling (818 mm/s, 26 BL/s), omnidirectional crawling, jumping, and swimming. Notably, the robot can fold to reduce its volume by 79%, enabling it to traverse confined spaces. We further validate its navigation capabilities on complex terrains, including discrete obstacles, viscoelastic gelatin surfaces, viscous fluids, and simulated biological tissues. This system offers a versatile strategy for developing high-mobility soft robots for future biomedical applications.

</details>


### [125] [Active Stereo-Camera Outperforms Multi-Sensor Setup in ACT Imitation Learning for Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28422)
*Robin Kühn,Moritz Schappler,Thomas Seel,Dennis Bank*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文通过系统评估14种传感器组合在Unitree G1人形机器人上的性能，发现在数据有限的情况下，主动立体视觉配置优于复杂多传感器系统，而触觉传感器因信噪比低反而降低成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人学习新任务的复杂性阻碍了其在工业中的广泛应用。虽然模仿学习（特别是ACT方法）能快速获取任务，但针对操作任务的最优传感器硬件配置尚无共识。需要评估不同传感器组合在数据有限情况下的性能，以指导高效学习系统的设计。

Method: 在Unitree G1人形机器人（配备三指手）上对14种传感器组合进行基准测试，评估两种操作任务。通过传感器掩码技术开发开源统一消融框架，在综合主数据集上评估触觉、本体感觉和主动视觉模态的集成效果。

Result: 策略性传感器选择在数据有限情况下优于复杂配置：最小主动立体视觉配置在空间泛化任务中达到87.5%成功率，在结构化操作任务中达到94.4%。相反，添加压力传感器使后者成功率降至67.3%（信噪比低）。额外模态在有限数据IL中常降低性能。

Conclusion: 在数据有限情况下，主动视觉在鲁棒性和复杂性之间提供了最佳权衡。触觉模态可能需要更大数据集才能有效，但策略性传感器选择对设计高效学习过程至关重要。复杂传感器配置不一定带来性能提升，需根据数据可用性进行优化选择。

Abstract: The complexity of teaching humanoid robots new tasks is one of the major reasons hindering their widespread adoption in the industry. While Imitation Learning (IL), particularly Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT), enables rapid task acquisition, there is no consensus yet on the optimal sensory hardware required for manipulation tasks. This paper benchmarks 14 sensor combinations on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot equipped with three-finger hands for two manipulation tasks. We explicitly evaluate the integration of tactile and proprioceptive modalities alongside active vision. Our analysis demonstrates that strategic sensor selection can outperform complex configurations in data-limited regimes while reducing computational overhead. We develop an open-source Unified Ablation Framework that utilizes sensor masking on a comprehensive master dataset. Results indicate that additional modalities often degrade performance for IL with limited data. A minimal active stereo-camera setup outperformed complex multi-sensor configurations, achieving 87.5% success in a spatial generalization task and 94.4% in a structured manipulation task. Conversely, adding pressure sensors to this setup reduced success to 67.3% in the latter task due to a low signal-to-noise ratio. We conclude that in data-limited regimes, active vision offers a superior trade-off between robustness and complexity. While tactile modalities may require larger datasets to be effective, our findings validate that strategic sensor selection is critical for designing an efficient learning process.

</details>


### [126] [Tele-Catch: Adaptive Teleoperation for Dexterous Dynamic 3D Object Catching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28427)
*Weiguang Zhao,Junting Dong,Rui Zhang,Kailin Li,Qin Zhao,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Tele-Catch框架通过DAIM机制和DP-U3R方法实现动态物体抓取的共享自主控制，显著提升抓取精度和鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态物体抓取任务在遥操作中研究不足，纯遥操作常因时序、位姿和力误差而失败，需要结合人类输入与自主策略的共享自主控制方法

Method: 提出Tele-Catch框架：1) DAIM机制将手套遥操作信号融合到扩散策略去噪过程，实现基于交互物体状态的自适应控制；2) DP-U3R将点云的无监督几何表示集成到扩散策略学习中，实现几何感知决策

Result: 实验表明Tele-Catch在动态抓取任务中显著提升准确性和鲁棒性，在不同灵巧手配置和未见物体类别上均表现出一致增益

Conclusion: Tele-Catch为动态物体抓取提供了有效的共享自主控制框架，通过自适应融合人类输入与自主策略，实现了对动态物体的精确鲁棒抓取

Abstract: Teleoperation is a key paradigm for transferring human dexterity to robots, yet most prior work targets objects that are initially static, such as grasping or manipulation. Dynamic object catch, where objects move before contact, remains underexplored. Pure teleoperation in this task often fails due to timing, pose, and force errors, highlighting the need for shared autonomy that combines human input with autonomous policies. To this end, we present Tele-Catch, a systematic framework for dexterous hand teleoperation in dynamic object catching. At its core, we design DAIM, a dynamics-aware adaptive integration mechanism that realizes shared autonomy by fusing glove-based teleoperation signals into the diffusion policy denoising process. It adaptively modulates control based on the interaction object state. To improve policy robustness, we introduce DP-U3R, which integrates unsupervised geometric representations from point cloud observations into diffusion policy learning, enabling geometry-aware decision making. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Tele-Catch significantly improves accuracy and robustness in dynamic catching tasks, while also exhibiting consistent gains across distinct dexterous hand embodiments and previously unseen object categories.

</details>


### [127] [A Predictive Control Strategy to Offset-Point Tracking for Agricultural Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28439)
*Stephane Ngnepiepaye Wembe,Vincent Rousseau,Johann Laconte,Roland Lenain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于阿克曼转向农业车辆的闭环预测控制策略，显式建模农具作为刚性偏移点，显著降低了跟踪误差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径跟踪控制器主要关注机器人运动中心，忽略了农具的空间足迹和动力学特性。农具通常体积大、刚性安装，直接与作物和土壤交互，忽略其位置会降低跟踪性能并增加作物损伤风险

Method: 提出闭环预测控制策略，扩展[1]的方法，专门针对阿克曼转向农业车辆开发。方法显式将农具建模为刚性偏移点，同时考虑横向滑移和杠杆臂效应

Result: 与最先进的基准控制器（包括反应几何方法、反应反步法和基于模型的预测方案）相比，真实农业实验显示：该方法将中位跟踪误差降低24%至56%，曲率转换期间的峰值误差降低高达70%

Conclusion: 该方法显著提高了农业机器人的跟踪性能，特别是在农具靠近作物行操作的场景中，增强了操作安全性

Abstract: Robots are increasingly being deployed in agriculture to support sustainable practices and improve productivity. They offer strong potential to enable precise, efficient, and environmentally friendly operations. However, most existing path-following controllers focus solely on the robot's center of motion and neglect the spatial footprint and dynamics of attached implements. In practice, implements such as mechanical weeders or spring-tine cultivators are often large, rigidly mounted, and directly interacting with crops and soil; ignoring their position can degrade tracking performance and increase the risk of crop damage. To address this limitation, we propose a closed-form predictive control strategy extending the approach introduced in [1]. The method is developed specifically for Ackermann-type agricultural vehicles and explicitly models the implement as a rigid offset point, while accounting for lateral slip and lever-arm effects. The approach is benchmarked against state-of-the-art baseline controllers, including a reactive geometric method, a reactive backstepping method, and a model-based predictive scheme. Real-world agricultural experiments with two different implements show that the proposed method reduces the median tracking error by 24% to 56%, and decreases peak errors during curvature transitions by up to 70%. These improvements translate into enhanced operational safety, particularly in scenarios where the implement operates in close proximity to crop rows.

</details>


### [128] [Communications-Aware NMPC for Multi-Rotor Aerial Relay Networks Under Jamming Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28467)
*Giuseppe Silano,Daniel Bonilla Licea,Davide Liuzza,Antonio Franchi,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通信感知的模块化控制框架，将轨迹优化与非线性模型预测控制结合，通过考虑天线方向性来提升多旋翼无人机在干扰环境下的通信可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼无人机在依赖通信的任务中，现有抗干扰策略通常将运动与通信解耦，忽略了链路质量受飞行器姿态和天线方向的影响。在共面平台中，"倾斜平移"机动可能无意中将天线零点对准通信伙伴，导致干扰下通信严重恶化。

Method: 提出了模块化的通信感知控制框架，包含高层最大-最小轨迹生成器和执行器级非线性模型预测控制器。轨迹层优化最弱链路在干扰下的性能，NMPC层强制执行飞行器动力学、执行器限制和天线对准约束。天线方向性通过几何方式处理，避免显式的辐射模式参数化。

Result: 在带有主动干扰器的中继场景中评估该方法，比较共面和倾斜螺旋桨架构。结果显示最小端到端容量提升近两个数量级，显著减少中断事件，平均容量增益适中。倾斜平台保持可行性和链路质量，而共面飞行器显示周期性恶化。

Conclusion: 完全驱动是实现可靠通信感知操作的关键使能因素，特别是在对抗性方向约束下。该框架通过整合通信约束与飞行控制，显著提升了多旋翼无人机在干扰环境下的通信可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-Rotor Aerial Vehicles (MRAVs) are increasingly used in communication-dependent missions where connectivity loss directly compromises task execution. Existing anti-jamming strategies often decouple motion from communication, overlooking that link quality depends on vehicle attitude and antenna orientation. In coplanar platforms, "tilt-to-translate" maneuvers can inadvertently align antenna nulls with communication partners, causing severe degradation under interference. This paper presents a modular communications-aware control framework that combines a high-level max-min trajectory generator with an actuator-level Nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (NMPC). The trajectory layer optimizes the weakest link under jamming, while the NMPC enforces vehicle dynamics, actuator limits, and antenna-alignment constraints. Antenna directionality is handled geometrically, avoiding explicit radiation-pattern parametrization. The method is evaluated in a relay scenario with an active jammer and compared across coplanar and tilted-propeller architectures. Results show a near two-order-of-magnitude increase in minimum end-to-end capacity, markedly reducing outage events, with moderate average-capacity gains. Tilted platforms preserve feasibility and link quality, whereas coplanar vehicles show recurrent degradation. These findings indicate that full actuation is a key enabler of reliable communications-aware operation under adversarial directional constraints.

</details>


### [129] [Tac2Real: Reliable and GPU Visuotactile Simulation for Online Reinforcement Learning and Zero-Shot Real-World Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28475)
*Ningyu Yan,Shuai Wang,Xing Shen,Hui Wang,Hanqing Wang,Yang Xiang,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Tac2Real是一个轻量级视觉触觉仿真框架，通过PNCG-IPC方法和多节点多GPU并行架构实现高效在线强化学习训练，结合TacAlign方法缩小仿真与现实的领域差距，在钉插入任务中实现零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感器对于接触丰富的机器人操作任务至关重要，但在仿真中进行触觉反馈的策略学习（特别是在线强化学习）面临重大挑战，需要在物理保真度和计算效率之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出Tac2Real框架：1）集成PNCG-IPC方法（预条件非线性共轭梯度增量势接触法）与多节点多GPU高吞吐量并行仿真架构，以交互速率生成标记位移场；2）提出TacAlign方法，系统性地缩小结构性和随机性领域差距。

Result: 在接触丰富的钉插入任务上进行评估，零样本迁移在真实场景中实现了高成功率，验证了框架的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Tac2Real框架成功解决了触觉仿真中物理保真度与计算效率的平衡问题，通过TacAlign方法实现了可靠的零样本仿真到现实迁移，为接触丰富的机器人操作任务提供了有效的训练解决方案。

Abstract: Visuotactile sensors are indispensable for contact-rich robotic manipulation tasks. However, policy learning with tactile feedback in simulation, especially for online reinforcement learning (RL), remains a critical challenge, as it demands a delicate balance between physics fidelity and computational efficiency. To address this challenge, we present Tac2Real, a lightweight visuotactile simulation framework designed to enable efficient online RL training. Tac2Real integrates the Preconditioned Nonlinear Conjugate Gradient Incremental Potential Contact (PNCG-IPC) method with a multi-node, multi-GPU high-throughput parallel simulation architecture, which can generate marker displacement fields at interactive rates. Meanwhile, we propose a systematic approach, TacAlign, to narrow both structured and stochastic sources of domain gap, ensuring a reliable zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. We further evaluate Tac2Real on the contact-rich peg insertion task. The zero-shot transfer results achieve a high success rate in the real-world scenario, verifying the effectiveness and robustness of our framework. The project page is: https://ningyurichard.github.io/tac2real-project-page/

</details>


### [130] [RAD-LAD: Rule and Language Grounded Autonomous Driving in Real-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28522)
*Anurag Ghosh,Srinivasa Narasimhan,Manmohan Chandraker,Francesco Pittaluga*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LAD是实时语言-动作规划器，RAD是基于规则的规划器，两者结合形成混合规划系统，在nuPlan基准测试中取得最先进性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自动驾驶规划中的实时性、可靠性和适应性问题，结合基于规则的方法和基于学习的方法的优势，提供可解释的决策能力

Method: LAD采用可中断架构，单次前向传递生成运动规划（20Hz）或带文本推理的运动规划（10Hz）；RAD是针对PDM-Closed结构限制设计的基于规则的规划器；两者结合形成混合规划系统

Result: LAD延迟比先前驾驶语言模型低3倍，在nuPlan Test14-Hard和InterPlan上达到基于学习的最新水平；RAD在基于规则的规划器中达到最先进性能；混合系统结合了两者优势

Conclusion: 规则和学习提供互补能力：规则支持可靠机动，语言实现自适应和可解释决策；混合规划系统能够同时利用两者的优势

Abstract: We present LAD, a real-time language--action planner with an interruptible architecture that produces a motion plan in a single forward pass (~20 Hz) or generates textual reasoning alongside a motion plan (~10 Hz). LAD is fast enough for real-time closed-loop deployment, achieving ~3x lower latency than prior driving language models while setting a new learning-based state of the art on nuPlan Test14-Hard and InterPlan. We also introduce RAD, a rule-based planner designed to address structural limitations of PDM-Closed. RAD achieves state-of-the-art performance among rule-based planners on nuPlan Test14-Hard and InterPlan. Finally, we show that combining RAD and LAD enables hybrid planning that captures the strengths of both approaches. This hybrid system demonstrates that rules and learning provide complementary capabilities: rules support reliable maneuvering, while language enables adaptive and explainable decision-making.

</details>


### [131] [Feel Robot Feels: Tactile Feedback Array Glove for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28542)
*Feiyu Jia,Xiaojie Niu,Sizhe Yang,Qingwei Ben,Tao Huang,Feng zhao,Jingbo Wang,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAG是一个低成本手套系统，集成了精确的手部运动捕捉和高分辨率触觉反馈，用于灵巧遥操作，解决了现有遥操作中运动映射不准确和触觉反馈有限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧遥操作面临两个主要限制：1) 不准确的手-机器人运动映射限制了遥操作的灵巧性；2) 有限的触觉反馈迫使操作者依赖视觉主导的交互，难以感知接触几何和力变化。这些限制影响了高质量演示数据的收集。

Method: TAG采用非接触式磁感应设计进行21自由度无漂移关节跟踪（关节角度误差<1度），同时在每个手指配备32个执行器的触觉阵列（2cm²紧凑模块），通过空间激活模式让操作者直接感受机器人末端执行器的物理交互。

Result: 通过真实世界遥操作实验和用户研究，TAG能够可靠实时感知接触几何和动态力，提高接触密集遥操作任务的成功率，并增强基于学习的操作演示数据收集的可靠性。

Conclusion: TAG系统通过集成精确运动捕捉和高分辨率触觉反馈，有效解决了灵巧遥操作的关键限制，为机器人操作提供了更有效的触觉闭环遥操作解决方案。

Abstract: Teleoperation is a key approach for collecting high-quality, physically consistent demonstrations for robotic manipulation. However, teleoperation for dexterous manipulation remains constrained by: (i) inaccurate hand-robot motion mapping, which limits teleoperated dexterity, and (ii) limited tactile feedback that forces vision-dominated interaction and hinders perception of contact geometry and force variation. To address these challenges, we present TAG, a low-cost glove system that integrates precise hand motion capture with high-resolution tactile feedback, enabling effective tactile-in-the-loop dexterous teleoperation. For motion capture, TAG employs a non-contact magnetic sensing design that provides drift-free, electromagnetically robust 21-DoF joint tracking with joint angle estimation errors below 1 degree. Meanwhile, to restore tactile sensation, TAG equips each finger with a 32-actuator tactile array within a compact 2 cm^2 module, allowing operators to directly feel physical interactions at the robot end-effector through spatial activation patterns. Through real-world teleoperation experiments and user studies, we show that TAG enables reliable real-time perception of contact geometry and dynamic force, improves success rates in contact-rich teleoperation tasks, and increases the reliability of demonstration data collection for learning-based manipulation.

</details>


### [132] [ManipArena: Comprehensive Real-world Evaluation of Reasoning-Oriented Generalist Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28545)
*Yu Sun,Meng Cao,Ping Yang,Rongtao Xu,Yunxiao Yan,Runze Xu,Liang Ma,Roy Gan,Andy Zhai,Qingxuan Chen,Zunnan Xu,Hao Wang,Jincheng Yu,Lucy Liang,Qian Wang,Ivan Laptev,Ian D Reid,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManipArena是一个标准化的机器人评估框架，旨在弥合仿真与真实世界执行的差距，为VLA模型和世界模型提供公平、真实、可重复的评估环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人评估基准主要基于仿真器，虽然可控但无法捕捉真实世界中的感知噪声、复杂接触动力学、硬件约束和系统延迟等现实差距。同时，不同机器人平台上的碎片化真实世界评估阻碍了公平和可重复的比较。

Method: ManipArena包含20个多样化任务，涵盖10,812条专家轨迹，强调需要语义和空间推理的推理导向操作任务。通过受控的分布外设置支持多级泛化，并超越桌面场景包含长时程移动操作。框架提供丰富的感官诊断（包括低级运动信号）和通过高质量3D扫描构建的同步真实到仿真环境。

Result: ManipArena为VLA模型和世界模型方法提供了公平、真实且可重复的评估框架，能够诊断和推进具身智能系统的发展。

Conclusion: ManipArena通过标准化评估框架解决了仿真与真实世界之间的差距问题，为机器人智能系统的评估提供了可扩展的基础，有助于推动VLA模型和世界模型在真实部署环境中的发展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and world models have recently emerged as promising paradigms for general-purpose robotic intelligence, yet their progress is hindered by the lack of reliable evaluation protocols that reflect real-world deployment. Existing benchmarks are largely simulator-centric, which provide controllability but fail to capture the reality gap caused by perception noise, complex contact dynamics, hardware constraints, and system latency. Moreover, fragmented real-world evaluations across different robot platforms prevent fair and reproducible comparison. To address these challenges, we introduce ManipArena, a standardized evaluation framework designed to bridge simulation and real-world execution. ManipArena comprises 20 diverse tasks across 10,812 expert trajectories emphasizing reasoning-oriented manipulation tasks requiring semantic and spatial reasoning, supports multi-level generalization through controlled out-of-distribution settings, and incorporates long-horizon mobile manipulation beyond tabletop scenarios. The framework further provides rich sensory diagnostics, including low-level motor signals, and synchronized real-to-sim environments constructed via high-quality 3D scanning. Together, these features enable fair, realistic, and reproducible evaluation for both VLA and world model approaches, providing a scalable foundation for diagnosing and advancing embodied intelligence systems.

</details>


### [133] [Fine-Tuning Large Language Models for Cooperative Tactical Deconfliction of Small Unmanned Aerial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28561)
*Iman Sharifi,Alex Zongo,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究使用大语言模型（LLMs）作为小型无人机系统（sUAS）战术冲突解脱的决策者，通过基于BlueSky模拟器的数据生成管道和两种参数高效微调策略（监督LoRA微调和GRPO偏好微调）来提升模型在密集、部分可观测、异构多智能体环境中的决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着小型无人机系统在低空空域的广泛应用，需要在安全关键约束下实现可靠的战术冲突解脱。战术冲突解脱涉及密集、部分可观测、异构的多智能体环境中的短期决策，需要同时保证合作分离安全和运行效率。虽然大语言模型展现出强大的推理能力，但直接应用于空中交通管制仍受限于领域知识不足和输出不一致的问题。

Method: 1. 提出基于BlueSky空中交通模拟器的模拟到语言数据生成管道，生成符合规则的安全实践冲突解脱数据集；2. 使用预训练的Qwen-Math-7B模型，采用两种参数高效微调策略：基于低秩适应（LoRA）的监督微调，以及结合LoRA与组相对策略优化（GRPO）的偏好微调；3. 在验证数据集和闭环模拟中进行实验评估。

Result: 监督LoRA微调相比预训练LLM显著提高了决策准确性、一致性和分离性能，大幅减少了近空中碰撞。GRPO提供了额外的协调优势，但在与异构智能体策略交互时表现出较低的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过领域特定的微调策略，大语言模型可以成为小型无人机系统战术冲突解脱的有效决策者。监督LoRA微调在提升安全性和一致性方面表现优异，而GRPO虽然能改善协调能力，但在异构环境中需要进一步优化鲁棒性。

Abstract: The growing deployment of small Unmanned Aerial Systems (sUASs) in low-altitude airspaces has increased the need for reliable tactical deconfliction under safety-critical constraints. Tactical deconfliction involves short-horizon decision-making in dense, partially observable, and heterogeneous multi-agent environments, where both cooperative separation assurance and operational efficiency must be maintained. While Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, their direct application to air traffic control remains limited by insufficient domain grounding and unpredictable output inconsistency. This paper investigates LLMs as decision-makers in cooperative multi-agent tactical deconfliction using fine-tuning strategies that align model outputs to human operator heuristics. We propose a simulation-to-language data generation pipeline based on the BlueSky air traffic simulator that produces rule-consistent deconfliction datasets reflecting established safety practices. A pretrained Qwen-Math-7B model is fine-tuned using two parameter-efficient strategies: supervised fine-tuning with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and preference-based fine-tuning combining LoRA with Group-Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Experimental results on validation datasets and closed-loop simulations demonstrate that supervised LoRA fine-tuning substantially improves decision accuracy, consistency, and separation performance compared to the pretrained LLM, with significant reductions in near mid-air collisions. GRPO provides additional coordination benefits but exhibits reduced robustness when interacting with heterogeneous agent policies.

</details>


### [134] [StreamingVLA: Streaming Vision-Language-Action Model with Action Flow Matching and Adaptive Early Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28565)
*Yiran Shi,Dongqi Guo,Tianchen Zhao,Feng Gao,Liangzhi Shi,Chao Yu,ZhiJian Mo,Qihua Xiao,XiaoShuai Peng,Qingmin Liao,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StreamingVLA提出了一种流式并行化方法，通过动作流匹配和自适应观察机制，在保持性能的同时显著降低VLA模型的延迟和执行停顿


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在自然语言驱动的感知和控制方面表现出色，但高计算成本在资源受限的边缘平台上带来效率挑战。由于VLA的不同阶段（观察、动作生成和执行）必须顺序执行，系统存在频繁停顿和高延迟问题

Method: 1. 采用动作流匹配替代动作分块：学习动作流的轨迹而非去噪分块动作，实现动作生成和执行的延迟重叠；2. 设计动作显著性感知的自适应观察机制：实现执行和观察的延迟重叠

Result: StreamingVLA在保持性能的同时实现了显著加速：2.4倍的延迟加速和6.5倍执行停顿减少

Conclusion: 提出的流式并行化方法通过重叠VLA各阶段的延迟，有效解决了传统VLA模型的效率瓶颈，实现了快速流畅的动作生成和执行

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated exceptional performance in natural language-driven perception and control. However, the high computational cost of VLA models poses significant efficiency challenges, particularly for resource-constrained edge platforms in real-world deployments. However, since different stages of VLA (observation, action generation and execution) must proceed sequentially, and wait for the completion of the preceding stage, the system suffers from frequent halting and high latency. To address this, We conduct a systematic analysis to identify the challenges for fast and fluent generation, and propose enabling VLAs with the ability to asynchronously parallelize across VLA stages in a "streaming" manner. First, we eliminate the reliance on action chunking and adopt action flow matching, which learns the trajectory of action flows rather than denoising chunk-wise actions. It overlaps the latency of action generation and execution. Second, we design an action saliency-aware adaptive observation mechanism, thereby overlapping the latency of execution and observation. Without sacrificing performance, StreamingVLA achieves substantial speedup and improves the fluency of execution. It achieves a 2.4 $\times$ latency speedup and reduces execution halting by 6.5 $\times$.

</details>


### [135] [A Self-Rotating Tri-Rotor UAV for Field of View Expansion and Autonomous Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28581)
*Xiaobin Zhou,Zihao Zheng,Aoxu Jin,Lei Qiang,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPINNER是一种自旋三旋翼无人机，通过持续旋转运动扩展机载传感器视场，无需额外传感器或能耗，同时实现三维位置和姿态控制，并在扰动环境下保持鲁棒飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机感知系统受限于机载传感器（如相机和LiDAR）的狭窄视场，限制了环境感知能力。需要一种在不增加额外传感器或能耗的情况下扩展视场的方法。

Method: 设计自旋三旋翼无人机SPINNER，通过持续旋转运动扩展传感器视场；采用三个无刷电机实现三维位置和滚转-俯仰姿态控制；通过反扭矩板设计调整旋转速度；开发结合非线性模型预测控制（MPC）和增量非线性动态反演的扰动补偿控制框架，处理旋转飞行引起的强耦合、严重非线性和复杂扰动。

Result: SPINNER在风速高达4.8 m/s的风扰动下保持鲁棒飞行；在最大速度2.0 m/s时实现高精度轨迹跟踪；在停车场和森林环境测试中，旋转感知机制显著提高了视场覆盖范围，增强了感知能力。

Conclusion: SPINNER通过创新的自旋设计有效扩展了无人机传感器的视场，同时实现了鲁棒的控制性能，为无人机在复杂环境中的感知和导航提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) perception relies on onboard sensors like cameras and LiDAR, which are limited by the narrow field of view (FoV). We present Self-Perception INertial Navigation Enabled Rotorcraft (SPINNER), a self-rotating tri-rotor UAV for the FoV expansion and autonomous flight. Without adding extra sensors or energy consumption, SPINNER significantly expands the FoV of onboard camera and LiDAR sensors through continuous spin motion, thereby enhancing environmental perception efficiency. SPINNER achieves full 3-dimensional position and roll--pitch attitude control using only three brushless motors, while adjusting the rotation speed via anti-torque plates design. To address the strong coupling, severe nonlinearity, and complex disturbances induced by spinning flight, we develop a disturbance compensation control framework that combines nonlinear model predictive control (MPC) with incremental nonlinear dynamic inversion. Experimental results demonstrate that SPINNER maintains robust flight under wind disturbances up to 4.8 \,m/s and achieves high-precision trajectory tracking at a maximum speed of 2.0\,m/s. Moreover, tests in parking garages and forests show that the rotational perception mechanism substantially improves FoV coverage and enhances perception capability of SPINNER.

</details>


### [136] [Dynamic Lookahead Distance via Reinforcement Learning-Based Pure Pursuit for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28625)
*Mohamed Elgouhary,Amr S. El-Wakeel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合控制框架，将PPO强化学习与经典Pure Pursuit控制器结合，动态调整前瞻距离以提升赛车路径跟踪性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pure Pursuit算法在自动驾驶车辆中广泛应用，但其性能对前瞻距离的选择非常敏感：较短距离改善转弯性能但可能导致直线不稳定，较长距离提高平滑度但降低曲线精度。需要一种能够动态调整前瞻距离的方法来平衡这些矛盾需求

Method: 提出混合控制框架，集成PPO强化学习与经典Pure Pursuit控制器。PPO智能体将车辆速度和多视野曲率特征映射到在线前瞻距离命令。使用Stable-Baselines3在F1TENTH Gym模拟器中训练，采用KL惩罚和学习率衰减确保稳定性，然后在ROS2环境中部署以指导控制器

Result: 在模拟实验中，与固定前瞻距离Pure Pursuit和自适应Pure Pursuit基线相比，学习策略提高了圈速性能和未见赛道的重复圈完成率。在蒙特利尔赛道达到33.16秒，亚斯码头赛道达到46.05秒，比基线容忍更激进的速档缩放，获得最佳圈速。初步实车实验支持模拟到现实的零样本迁移

Conclusion: 学习控制器通过增加直线前瞻距离和减少曲线前瞻距离来适应不同路况，证明了通过在线调整单个可解释参数来增强经典控制器的有效性。该方法在未见赛道上表现出色，并成功实现模拟到现实的迁移

Abstract: Pure Pursuit (PP) is a widely used path-tracking algorithm in autonomous vehicles due to its simplicity and real-time performance. However, its effectiveness is sensitive to the choice of lookahead distance: shorter values improve cornering but can cause instability on straights, while longer values improve smoothness but reduce accuracy in curves. We propose a hybrid control framework that integrates Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with the classical Pure Pursuit controller to adjust the lookahead distance dynamically during racing. The PPO agent maps vehicle speed and multi-horizon curvature features to an online lookahead command. It is trained using Stable-Baselines3 in the F1TENTH Gym simulator with a KL penalty and learning-rate decay for stability, then deployed in a ROS2 environment to guide the controller. Experiments in simulation compare the proposed method against both fixed-lookahead Pure Pursuit and an adaptive Pure Pursuit baseline. Additional real-car experiments compare the learned controller against a fixed-lookahead Pure Pursuit controller. Results show that the learned policy improves lap-time performance and repeated lap completion on unseen tracks, while also transferring zero-shot to hardware. The learned controller adapts the lookahead by increasing it on straights and reducing it in curves, demonstrating effectiveness in augmenting a classical controller by online adaptation of a single interpretable parameter. On unseen tracks, the proposed method achieved 33.16 s on Montreal and 46.05 s on Yas Marina, while tolerating more aggressive speed-profile scaling than the baselines and achieving the best lap times among the tested settings. Initial real-car experiments further support sim-to-real transfer on a 1:10-scale autonomous racing platform

</details>


### [137] [Serialized Red-Green-Gray: Quicker Heuristic Validation of Edges in Dynamic Roadmap Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28674)
*Yulie Arad,Stav Ashur,Marta Markowicz,James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RGG框架结合SPITE快速分类路图边为无效、有效或不确定，SerRGG通过批处理和向量化实现GPU加速，在动态环境中比顺序实现快2-9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境（如机器人仓库）中的运动规划需要快速适应障碍物姿态的频繁变化。传统基于路图的方法在此类场景中表现不佳，要么依赖低效的路图重建，要么需要昂贵的碰撞检测来更新现有路图。

Method: 提出Red-Green-Gray（RGG）框架，基于SPITE方法，使用保守几何近似快速将路图边分类为无效（红色）、有效（绿色）或不确定（灰色）。SerRGG是高性能变体，利用批处理序列化和向量化实现GPU加速。

Result: RGG有效减少了需要完全验证的未知边数量，SerRGG相比顺序实现实现了2-9倍的加速。这种几何精度和计算速度的结合使SerRGG在时间关键的机器人应用中非常有效。

Conclusion: RGG框架通过快速边分类和GPU加速，解决了动态环境中传统路图方法的效率问题，为时间关键的机器人应用提供了高效的运动规划解决方案。

Abstract: Motion planning in dynamic environments, such as robotic warehouses, requires fast adaptation to frequent changes in obstacle poses. Traditional roadmap-based methods struggle in such settings, relying on inefficient reconstruction of a roadmap or expensive collision detection to update the existing roadmap. To address these challenges we introduce the Red-Green-Gray (RGG) framework, a method that builds on SPITE to quickly classify roadmap edges as invalid (red), valid (green), or uncertain (gray) using conservative geometric approximations. Serial RGG provides a high-performance variant leveraging batch serialization and vectorization to enable efficient GPU acceleration. Empirical results demonstrate that while RGG effectively reduces the number of unknown edges requiring full validation, SerRGG achieves a 2-9x speedup compared to the sequential implementation. This combination of geometric precision and computational speed makes SerRGG highly effective for time-critical robotic applications.

</details>


### [138] [DRIVE-Nav: Directional Reasoning, Inspection, and Verification for Efficient Open-Vocabulary Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28691)
*Maoguo Gao,Zejun Zhu,Zhiming Sun,Zhengwei Ma,Longze Yuan,Zhongjing Ma,Zhigang Gao,Jinhui Zhang,Suli Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIVE-Nav是一个用于开放词汇目标导航的结构化框架，通过基于方向而非原始边界点组织探索，减少重复访问并提高路径效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本方法在不完整观测下基于密集边界点进行推理，导致路线选择不稳定、重复访问和额外动作开销，需要更稳定的探索策略

Method: 提出结构化框架，围绕持久方向而非原始边界组织探索；使用加权快速行进法提取和跟踪方向候选；维护代表性视图进行语义检查；结合视觉语言引导的提示增强和跨帧验证提高接地可靠性；将后续决策限制在前方240度视野范围内仍相关的方向

Result: 在HM3D-OVON上达到50.2% SR和32.6% SPL，比之前最佳方法提升1.9% SR和5.6% SPL；在HM3Dv2和MP3D上获得最佳SPL；成功迁移到物理人形机器人并在真实世界部署中证明有效性

Conclusion: DRIVE-Nav通过结构化方向组织探索显著提高了开放词汇目标导航的效率和可靠性，减少了冗余访问并改善了路径效率，在多个基准测试中表现出色且具有实际部署能力

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation (OVON) requires an embodied agent to locate a language-specified target in unknown environments. Existing zero-shot methods often reason over dense frontier points under incomplete observations, causing unstable route selection, repeated revisits, and unnecessary action overhead. We present DRIVE-Nav, a structured framework that organizes exploration around persistent directions rather than raw frontiers. By inspecting encountered directions more completely and restricting subsequent decisions to still-relevant directions within a forward 240 degree view range, DRIVE-Nav reduces redundant revisits and improves path efficiency. The framework extracts and tracks directional candidates from weighted Fast Marching Method (FMM) paths, maintains representative views for semantic inspection, and combines vision-language-guided prompt enrichment with cross-frame verification to improve grounding reliability. Experiments on HM3D-OVON, HM3Dv2, and MP3D demonstrate strong overall performance and consistent efficiency gains. On HM3D-OVON, DRIVE-Nav achieves 50.2% SR and 32.6% SPL, improving the previous best method by 1.9% SR and 5.6% SPL. It also delivers the best SPL on HM3Dv2 and MP3D and transfers to a physical humanoid robot. Real-world deployment also demonstrates its effectiveness. Project page: https://coolmaoguo.github.io/drive-nav-page/

</details>


### [139] [SOLE-R1: Video-Language Reasoning as the Sole Reward for On-Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28730)
*Philip Schroeder,Thomas Weng,Karl Schmeckpeper,Eric Rosen,Stephen Hart,Ondrej Biza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOLE-R1是一个专门设计的视频语言推理模型，能够作为在线强化学习的唯一奖励信号，通过时空思维链推理生成密集的任务进度估计，实现零样本在线RL学习未见过的机器人操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最强的视觉语言模型在强化学习中作为评估器时，在部分可观测性和分布偏移下容易失败，导致策略利用感知错误而非真正解决任务。需要专门设计能够作为RL唯一奖励信号的模型。

Method: 开发SOLE-R1模型，通过大规模视频轨迹和推理合成管道生成时间基础的思维链轨迹，结合基础空间和多帧时间推理能力，采用监督微调与可验证奖励的强化学习混合框架进行训练。

Result: 在四个不同仿真环境和真实机器人设置中，SOLE-R1实现了从随机初始化的零样本在线RL，成功学习24个未见过的操作任务，显著优于GPT-5和Gemini-3-Pro等强视觉语言奖励模型，且对奖励攻击具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SOLE-R1通过专门的视频语言推理架构和训练方法，能够作为可靠的强化学习奖励信号，在零样本设置下实现有效的机器人任务学习，解决了现有VLMs在RL中作为评估器的局限性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, motivating efforts to leverage these models to supervise robot learning. However, when used as evaluators in reinforcement learning (RL), today's strongest models often fail under partial observability and distribution shift, enabling policies to exploit perceptual errors rather than solve the task. To address this limitation, we introduce SOLE-R1 (Self-Observing LEarner), a video-language reasoning model explicitly designed to serve as the sole reward signal for online RL. Given only raw video observations and a natural-language goal, SOLE-R1 performs per-timestep spatiotemporal chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and produces dense estimates of task progress that can be used directly as rewards. To train SOLE-R1, we develop a large-scale video trajectory and reasoning synthesis pipeline that generates temporally grounded CoT traces aligned with continuous progress supervision. This data is combined with foundational spatial and multi-frame temporal reasoning, and used to train the model with a hybrid framework that couples supervised fine-tuning with RL from verifiable rewards. Across four different simulation environments and a real-robot setting, SOLE-R1 enables zero-shot online RL from random initialization: robots learn previously unseen manipulation tasks without ground-truth rewards, success indicators, demonstrations, or task-specific tuning. SOLE-R1 succeeds on 24 unseen tasks and substantially outperforms strong vision-language rewarders, including GPT-5 and Gemini-3-Pro, while exhibiting markedly greater robustness to reward hacking.

</details>


### [140] [Pandora: Articulated 3D Scene Graphs from Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28732)
*Alan Yu,Yun Chang,Christopher Xie,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用人类佩戴Project Aria眼镜采集的自我中心数据，为机器人构建包含可动部件模型的3D场景图，提升机器人移动操作能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人建图系统受限于机器人自身传感器和技能，无法探索环境中的所有方面（如无法打开抽屉或壁柜），导致地图不完整。需要更强大的机器人来填补这些空白。

Method: 利用人类自然探索场景时佩戴Project Aria眼镜采集的自我中心数据，通过简单启发式方法恢复可动物体部件模型，并将其集成到3D场景图表示中，从而理解物体动态和物体-容器关系。

Result: 使用自我中心数据恢复的可动物体部件模型质量与基于其他输入模态的最先进方法相当。构建的关节化3D场景图增强了机器人执行移动操作任务的能力，在波士顿动力Spot机器人检索隐藏目标物品的任务中展示了应用效果。

Conclusion: 通过人类自我中心数据填补机器人建图的盲点，能够构建更完整的关节化3D场景图表示，显著提升机器人对动态物体和物体-容器关系的理解，从而增强移动操作任务的执行能力。

Abstract: Robotic mapping systems typically approach building metric-semantic scene representations from the robot's own sensors and cameras. However, these "first person" maps inherit the robot's own limitations due to its embodiment or skillset, which may leave many aspects of the environment unexplored. For example, the robot might not be able to open drawers or access wall cabinets. In this sense, the map representation is not as complete, and requires a more capable robot to fill in the gaps. We narrow these blind spots in current methods by leveraging egocentric data captured as a human naturally explores a scene wearing Project Aria glasses, giving a way to directly transfer knowledge about articulation from the human to any deployable robot. We demonstrate that, by using simple heuristics, we can leverage egocentric data to recover models of articulate object parts, with quality comparable to those of state-of-the-art methods based on other input modalities. We also show how to integrate these models into 3D scene graph representations, leading to a better understanding of object dynamics and object-container relationships. We finally demonstrate that these articulated 3D scene graphs enhance a robot's ability to perform mobile manipulation tasks, showcasing an application where a Boston Dynamics Spot is tasked with retrieving concealed target items, given only the 3D scene graph as input.

</details>


### [141] [FocusVLA: Focused Visual Utilization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28740)
*Yichi Zhang,Weihao Yuan,Yizhuo Zhang,Xidong Zhang,Jia Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FocusVLA通过模态级联注意力消除捷径路径，并引入聚焦注意力动态选择任务相关视觉区域，解决VLA模型在动作生成中忽视视觉细节、注意力分散和噪声干扰的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在动作生成中存在三个瓶颈：1）架构偏差导致模型忽视视觉细节；2）过多的视觉标记使注意力难以聚焦正确区域；3）任务无关的视觉信息引入大量噪声。这些因素严重影响了动作生成质量。

Method: 提出FocusVLA范式，包含两个核心组件：1）模态级联注意力，消除捷径路径，强制VLA模型依赖任务相关的视觉细节进行动作生成；2）聚焦注意力，动态选择任务相关的视觉补丁，控制信息量并显式调制其影响以抑制任务无关噪声。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界机器人基准测试上的广泛实验表明，FocusVLA不仅有效利用视觉细节执行灵巧操作，还在各种任务中显著提升性能并加速收敛。

Conclusion: VLA性能主要受视觉信息利用方式的限制，而非视觉表示质量。FocusVLA通过引导模型注意力到任务相关视觉区域，有效桥接视觉与动作，解决了当前VLA模型的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models improve action generation by conditioning policies on rich vision-language information. However, current auto-regressive policies are constrained by three bottlenecks: (1) architectural bias drives models to overlook visual details, (2) an excessive number of visual tokens makes attention difficult to focus on the correct regions, and (3) task-irrelevant visual information introduces substantial noise - together severely impairing the quality of action. In this paper, we investigate how to effectively utilize different visual representations for action generation. To this end, we first empirically validate the above issues and show that VLA performance is primarily limited by how visual information is utilized, rather than by the quality of visual representations. Based on these insights, we introduce FocusVLA, a novel paradigm that directs the model's attention to task-relevant visual regions to effectively bridge vision to action. Specifically, we first propose Modality Cascaded Attention to eliminate shortcut pathways, thereby compelling VLA models to rely on task-relevant visual details for action generation. Furthermore, we propose Focus Attention, which dynamically selects task-relevant visual patches to control information quantity while explicitly modulating their influence to suppress task-irrelevant noise. Extensive experiments on both simulated and real-world robotic benchmarks demonstrate that FocusVLA not only effectively leverages visual details to perform dexterous manipulations, but also substantially improves performance and accelerates convergence across a variety of tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Self-evolving AI agents for protein discovery and directed evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27303)
*Yang Tan,Lingrong Zhang,Mingchen Li,Yuanxi Yu,Bozitao Zhong,Bingxin Zhou,Nanqing Dong,Liang Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VenusFactory2是一个自主蛋白质科学发现框架，通过自演化的多智能体基础设施实现动态工作流合成，显著优于现有智能体，并能从单一自然语言提示自主组织蛋白质发现与优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质科学发现受限于人工编排信息和算法，而通用智能体在复杂领域项目中表现不足，需要更专业的自主框架来解决蛋白质相关需求

Method: 开发了VenusFactory2自主框架，从静态工具使用转向动态工作流合成，采用自演化的多智能体基础设施来应对蛋白质相关任务

Result: 在VenusAgentEval基准测试中优于一系列知名智能体，能够从单一自然语言提示自主组织蛋白质的发现和优化过程

Conclusion: VenusFactory2通过动态工作流合成和自演化多智能体基础设施，为蛋白质科学发现提供了有效的自主解决方案，显著提升了自动化水平和性能

Abstract: Protein scientific discovery is bottlenecked by the manual orchestration of information and algorithms, while general agents are insufficient in complex domain projects. VenusFactory2 provides an autonomous framework that shifts from static tool usage to dynamic workflow synthesis via a self-evolving multi-agent infrastructure to address protein-related demands. It outperforms a set of well-known agents on the VenusAgentEval benchmark, and autonomously organizes the discovery and optimization of proteins from a single natural language prompt.

</details>


### [143] [From indicators to biology: the calibration problem in artificial consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27597)
*Florentin Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文批评当前基于架构指标的人工意识评估方法，认为在缺乏独立验证和真实基准的情况下，这种概率性意识归因为时过早，主张转向基于生物系统的工程方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人工意识研究从行为测试转向架构指标评估，但这种方法存在根本性缺陷：意识科学理论碎片化、指标缺乏独立验证、没有人工现象性的真实基准，导致意识归因不可靠。

Method: 批判性分析当前人工意识评估方法的局限性，提出转向生物基础工程策略，包括生物混合系统、神经形态计算和连接组尺度系统，以缩小与生物系统的差距。

Result: 指出当前基于指标的概率性意识归因方法在认识论上校准不足，在现有条件下对当前AI系统进行意识归因为时过早，需要更可靠的替代方案。

Conclusion: 建议将研究重点转向生物基础工程方法，通过生物混合、神经形态和连接组尺度系统，在唯一具有经验基础的生物系统中建立人工意识研究的可靠基础。

Abstract: Recent work on artificial consciousness shifts evaluation from behaviour to internal architecture, deriving indicators from theories of consciousness and updating credences accordingly. This is progress beyond naive Turing-style tests. But the indicator-based programme remains epistemically under-calibrated: consciousness science is theoretically fragmented, indicators lack independent validation, and no ground truth of artificial phenomenality exists. Under these conditions, probabilistic consciousness attribution to current AI systems is premature. A more defensible near-term strategy is to redirect effort toward biologically grounded engineering -- biohybrid, neuromorphic, and connectome-scale systems -- that reduces the gap with the only domain where consciousness is empirically anchored: living systems.

</details>


### [144] [Multiverse: Language-Conditioned Multi-Game Level Blending via Shared Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26782)
*In-Chang Baek,Jiyun Jung,Sung-Hyun Kim,Geum-Hwan Hwang,Kyung-Joong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Multiverse是一个语言条件多游戏关卡生成器，通过文本规范实现跨游戏关卡混合，学习对齐文本指令和关卡结构的共享潜在空间，支持可控的跨游戏内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到关卡生成器通常局限于单一游戏领域，需要扩展语言条件生成到多个游戏，这需要学习能够捕捉跨领域结构关系的表示。

Method: 提出Multiverse模型，学习对齐文本指令和关卡结构的共享潜在空间，采用基于阈值的多正例对比监督连接跨游戏的语义相关关卡，支持通过潜在插值和组合文本提示进行可控混合。

Result: 实验表明，学习的表示支持可控的跨游戏关卡混合，显著提高了同一游戏类型内的混合质量，同时为语言条件多游戏内容生成提供了统一表示。

Conclusion: Multiverse通过共享潜在空间和对比监督实现了语言引导的跨游戏关卡混合，为多游戏文本到关卡生成提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Text-to-level generation aims to translate natural language descriptions into structured game levels, enabling intuitive control over procedural content generation. While prior text-to-level generators are typically limited to a single game domain, extending language-conditioned generation to multiple games requires learning representations that capture structural relationships across domains. We propose Multiverse, a language-conditioned multi-game level generator that enables cross-game level blending through textual specifications. The model learns a shared latent space aligning textual instructions and level structures, while a threshold-based multi-positive contrastive supervision links semantically related levels across games. This representation allows language to guide which structural characteristics should be preserved when combining content from different games, enabling controllable blending through latent interpolation and zero-shot generation from compositional textual prompts. Experiments show that the learned representation supports controllable cross-game level blending and significantly improves blending quality within the same game genre, while providing a unified representation for language-conditioned multi-game content generation.

</details>


### [145] [What does a system modify when it modifies itself?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27611)
*Florentin Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个区分认知系统自我修改目标的正式框架，识别了四种修改机制，揭示了人类与AI在自我修改结构上的交叉不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学和人工智能领域都研究系统的自我修改能力，但缺乏一个正式框架来区分不同类型的修改目标（如低级规则、控制规则或评估规范）。需要统一的理论框架来比较生物认知和人工系统的自我修改机制。

Method: 提出一个最小结构框架，包含规则层次、固定核心，并区分有效规则、表征规则和因果可访问规则。基于此框架识别了四种修改机制：无修改行动、低级修改、结构修改和目的论修改。将框架应用于人类认知和人工系统进行比较分析。

Result: 发现人类与AI在自我修改结构上存在交叉不对称性：人类在高层级有丰富的自我表征和因果能力，但操作层级不透明；而AI在操作层级有丰富的表征和因果访问，但最高评估层级缺乏这些能力。框架还能解释人工意识理论，并提出了四个可检验的预测和四个开放问题。

Conclusion: 该框架为比较生物认知和人工系统的自我修改能力提供了统一的理论基础，揭示了结构上的根本差异，并为理解人工意识、自主性和系统身份等核心问题提供了新的视角。

Abstract: When a cognitive system modifies its own functioning, what exactly does it modify: a low-level rule, a control rule, or the norm that evaluates its own revisions? Cognitive science describes executive control, metacognition, and hierarchical learning with precision, but lacks a formal framework distinguishing these targets of transformation. Contemporary artificial intelligence likewise exhibits self-modification without common criteria for comparison with biological cognition.
  We show that the question of what counts as a self-modifying system entails a minimal structure: a hierarchy of rules, a fixed core, and a distinction between effective rules, represented rules, and causally accessible rules. Four regimes are identified: (1) action without modification, (2) low-level modification, (3) structural modification, and (4) teleological revision. Each regime is anchored in a cognitive phenomenon and a corresponding artificial system.
  Applied to humans, the framework yields a central result: a crossing of opacities. Humans have self-representation and causal power concentrated at upper hierarchical levels, while operational levels remain largely opaque. Reflexive artificial systems display the inverse profile: rich representation and causal access at operational levels, but none at the highest evaluative level. This crossed asymmetry provides a structural signature for human-AI comparison. The framework also offers insight into artificial consciousness, with higher-order theories and Attention Schema Theory as special cases. We derive four testable predictions and identify four open problems: the independence of transformativity and autonomy, the viability of self-modification, the teleological lock, and identity under transformation.

</details>


### [146] [Concerning Uncertainty -- A Systematic Survey of Uncertainty-Aware XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26838)
*Helena Löfström,Tuwe Löfström,Anders Hjort,Fatima Rabia Yapicioglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文综述了不确定性感知的可解释人工智能（UAXAI），分析了不确定性如何融入解释流程、相关方法及其评估实践，提出了统一评估原则的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前不确定性感知的可解释AI领域缺乏系统性的综述，评估方法分散且以模型为中心，需要建立连接不确定性传播、鲁棒性和人类决策的统一评估框架。

Method: 通过文献调研，识别出三种主要的不确定性量化方法（贝叶斯、蒙特卡洛、保形方法）和三种不确定性融入解释的策略（评估可信度、约束模型/解释、显式传达不确定性），分析评估实践的现状与不足。

Result: 发现评估实践碎片化且主要关注模型层面，对用户关注有限，可靠性属性报告不一致；近期研究趋向于校准、分布无关技术，并将解释器变异性作为核心关注点。

Conclusion: UAXAI的进展需要统一评估原则，连接不确定性传播、鲁棒性和人类决策；反事实和校准方法是有望将可解释性与可靠性对齐的途径。

Abstract: This paper surveys uncertainty-aware explainable artificial intelligence (UAXAI), examining how uncertainty is incorporated into explanatory pipelines and how such methods are evaluated. Across the literature, three recurring approaches to uncertainty quantification emerge (Bayesian, Monte Carlo, and Conformal methods), alongside distinct strategies for integrating uncertainty into explanations: assessing trustworthiness, constraining models or explanations, and explicitly communicating uncertainty. Evaluation practices remain fragmented and largely model centered, with limited attention to users and inconsistent reporting of reliability properties (e.g., calibration, coverage, explanation stability). Recent work leans towards calibration, distribution free techniques and recognizes explainer variability as a central concern. We argue that progress in UAXAI requires unified evaluation principles that link uncertainty propagation, robustness, and human decision-making, and highlight counterfactual and calibration approaches as promising avenues for aligning interpretability with reliability.

</details>


### [147] [Transparency as Architecture: Structural Compliance Gaps in EU AI Act Article 50 II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26983)
*Vera Schmitt,Niklas Kruse,Premtim Sahitaj,Julius Schöning*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 欧盟AI法案第50条II要求AI生成内容进行双重透明度标记，但当前生成式AI系统存在结构性障碍，无法通过事后标记实现合规


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案第50条II要求AI生成内容必须同时具备人类可读和机器可读的透明度标记，但这一要求与当前生成式AI系统的技术限制存在根本冲突。论文旨在分析这种合规性挑战，特别是在合成数据生成和自动事实核查这两个诊断用例中

Method: 使用合成数据生成和自动事实核查作为诊断用例进行分析。通过这两个具体应用场景，展示当前AI系统在满足双重透明度要求时面临的技术障碍和结构性限制

Result: 研究发现合规性不能简化为事后标记。在事实核查中，迭代编辑工作流程和非确定性LLM输出使得溯源追踪不可行；在合成数据生成中，持久双重模式标记存在悖论：适合人类检查的水印可能在训练中被学习为虚假特征，而适合机器验证的标记在标准数据处理中很脆弱。存在三个结构性障碍：跨平台标记格式缺失、监管的"可靠性"标准与概率模型行为不匹配、以及缺乏针对不同用户专业水平的披露指导

Conclusion: 解决这些差距需要将透明度视为架构设计需求，需要跨学科研究，涵盖法律语义、AI工程和以人为中心的设计。合规性不能仅通过技术修复实现，而需要系统性的方法重新设计

Abstract: Art. 50 II of the EU Artificial Intelligence Act mandates dual transparency for AI-generated content: outputs must be labeled in both human-understandable and machine-readable form for automated verification. This requirement, entering into force in August 2026, collides with fundamental constraints of current generative AI systems. Using synthetic data generation and automated fact-checking as diagnostic use cases, we show that compliance cannot be reduced to post-hoc labeling. In fact-checking pipelines, provenance tracking is not feasible under iterative editorial workflows and non-deterministic LLM outputs; moreover, the assistive-function exemption does not apply, as such systems actively assign truth values rather than supporting editorial presentation. In synthetic data generation, persistent dual-mode marking is paradoxical: watermarks surviving human inspection risk being learned as spurious features during training, while marks suited for machine verification are fragile under standard data processing. Across both domains, three structural gaps obstruct compliance: (a) absent cross-platform marking formats for interleaved human-AI outputs; (b) misalignment between the regulation's 'reliability' criterion and probabilistic model behavior; and (c) missing guidance for adapting disclosures to heterogeneous user expertise. Closing these gaps requires transparency to be treated as an architectural design requirement, demanding interdisciplinary research across legal semantics, AI engineering, and human-centered desi

</details>


### [148] [When Verification Hurts: Asymmetric Effects of Multi-Agent Feedback in Logic Proof Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27076)
*Tahreem Yasir,Sutapa Dey Tithi,Benyamin Tabarsi,Dmitri Droujkov,Sam Gilson Yasitha Rajapaksha,Xiaoyi Tian,Arun Ramesh,DongKuan,Xu,Tiffany Barnes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了LLM在命题逻辑证明辅导中的可靠性，发现验证机制在反馈准确率低时有效，但在反馈已可靠时反而降低性能4-6个百分点，所有模型在复杂度4-5以上都表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地用于自动化辅导，但它们在结构化符号领域（如命题逻辑证明）的可靠性尚不清楚。需要评估LLM在需要精确符号推理、与学习者当前证明状态对齐的辅导任务中的表现。

Method: 1. 引入基于知识图谱的基准测试，包含516个独特证明状态，带有步骤级标注和难度指标；2. 设计三种角色专业化流程：Tutor（部分解决方案访问）、Teacher（完整推导访问）、Judge（验证Tutor反馈）；3. 使用细粒度分析框架，对照已验证的解决方案路径评估反馈质量。

Result: 1. 发现显著不对称性：当上游反馈错误率高（准确率<70%）时，验证能改善结果；但当反馈已可靠（准确率>85%）时，验证反而通过过度指定降低性能4-6个百分点；2. 识别共享复杂度上限：所有模型和流程在复杂度4-5以上的证明状态都无法可靠成功；3. 验证机制并非普遍有效。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了"添加验证器或更丰富上下文能普遍改善辅导"的假设，表明需要自适应、难度感知的架构，能够根据估计的复杂度和上游可靠性来路由问题，而不是采用一刀切的验证方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated tutoring, but their reliability in structured symbolic domains remains unclear. We study step-level feedback for propositional logic proofs, which require precise symbolic reasoning aligned with a learner's current proof state. We introduce a knowledge-graph-grounded benchmark of 516 unique proof states with step-level annotations and difficulty metrics. Unlike prior tutoring evaluations that rely on model self-assessment or binary correctness, our framework enables fine-grained analysis of feedback quality against verified solution paths. We evaluate three role-specialized pipelines with varying solution access: Tutor (partial solution access), Teacher (full derivation access), and Judge (verification of Tutor feedback). Our results reveal a striking asymmetry: verification improves outcomes when upstream feedback is error-prone (<70% accuracy), but degrades performance by 4-6 percentage points through over-specification when feedback is already reliable (>85%). Critically, we identify a shared complexity ceiling; no model or pipeline reliably succeeds on proof states exceeding complexity 4-5. These findings challenge the assumption that adding verifiers or richer context universally improves tutoring, motivating adaptive, difficulty-aware architectures that route problems by estimated complexity and upstream reliability.

</details>


### [149] [The Price of Meaning: Why Every Semantic Memory System Forgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27116)
*Sambartha Ray Barman,Andrey Starenky,Sofia Bodnar,Nikhil Narasimhan,Ashwin Gopinath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文证明语义记忆系统存在根本性权衡：语义组织促进泛化但必然导致干扰、遗忘和虚假回忆，无法通过架构设计完全避免


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI记忆系统普遍按语义组织信息以实现泛化和类比检索，但作者发现这种语义组织必然带来干扰、遗忘和虚假回忆等负面效应，需要系统性地分析这一根本权衡

Method: 提出"语义连续核阈值记忆"的形式化框架，在该类系统中推导四个理论结果，并在五种架构（向量检索、图记忆、注意力上下文、BM25文件系统检索、参数记忆）上验证预测

Result: 理论推导表明：语义有用表示具有有限有效秩；有限局部维度导致检索邻域存在正竞争质量；记忆增长时保留率衰减至零；满足δ-凸性条件的关联诱饵无法通过阈值调整消除虚假回忆。实验验证了这些预测

Conclusion: 意义的代价是干扰，所有测试架构都无法避免这一权衡。纯语义系统直接表现为遗忘和虚假回忆；推理增强系统部分缓解症状但将渐进退化转化为灾难性失败；完全避免干扰的系统则牺牲了语义泛化能力

Abstract: Every major AI memory system in production today organises information by meaning. That organisation enables generalisation, analogy, and conceptual retrieval -- but it comes at a price. We prove that the same geometric structure enabling semantic generalisation makes interference, forgetting, and false recall inescapable. We formalise this tradeoff for \textit{semantically continuous kernel-threshold memories}: systems whose retrieval score is a monotone function of an inner product in a semantic feature space with finite local intrinsic dimension.
  Within this class we derive four results: (1) semantically useful representations have finite effective rank; (2) finite local dimension implies positive competitor mass in retrieval neighbourhoods; (3) under growing memory, retention decays to zero, yielding power-law forgetting curves under power-law arrival statistics; (4) for associative lures satisfying a $δ$-convexity condition, false recall cannot be eliminated by threshold tuning.
  We test these predictions across five architectures: vector retrieval, graph memory, attention-based context, BM25 filesystem retrieval, and parametric memory. Pure semantic systems express the vulnerability directly as forgetting and false recall. Reasoning-augmented systems partially override these symptoms but convert graceful degradation into catastrophic failure. Systems that escape interference entirely do so by sacrificing semantic generalisation.
  The price of meaning is interference, and no architecture we tested avoids paying it.

</details>


### [150] [MediHive: A Decentralized Agent Collective for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27150)
*Xiaoyang Wang,Christopher C. Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MediHive是一个用于医学问答的去中心化多智能体框架，通过共享内存池和迭代融合机制，让LLM智能体自主分配角色、进行辩论并达成共识，在MedQA和PubMedQA数据集上优于单智能体和集中式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体LLM系统在处理复杂、跨学科的医学推理问题时存在局限，而现有的集中式多智能体系统存在可扩展性瓶颈、单点故障和角色混淆问题。去中心化多智能体系统在医疗领域应用不足，需要解决这些限制。

Method: 提出MediHive框架：1) 部署基于LLM的智能体自主分配专业角色；2) 进行初步分析；3) 通过条件性基于证据的辩论检测分歧；4) 在多轮中本地融合同伴见解达成共识；5) 集成共享内存池和迭代融合机制。

Result: 在MedQA数据集上达到84.3%准确率，在PubMedQA数据集上达到78.4%准确率，优于单LLM和集中式基线模型。证明了去中心化多智能体系统在医学推理任务中的优越性能。

Conclusion: MediHive推进了医疗AI领域可扩展、容错的去中心化多智能体系统，解决了集中式设计的关键限制，在推理密集型任务中展现出卓越性能，为高风险的医疗领域应用提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have revolutionized medical reasoning tasks, yet single-agent systems often falter on complex, interdisciplinary problems requiring robust handling of uncertainty and conflicting evidence. Multi-agent systems (MAS) leveraging LLMs enable collaborative intelligence, but prevailing centralized architectures suffer from scalability bottlenecks, single points of failure, and role confusion in resource-constrained environments. Decentralized MAS (D-MAS) promise enhanced autonomy and resilience via peer-to-peer interactions, but their application to high-stakes healthcare domains remains underexplored. We introduce MediHive, a novel decentralized multi-agent framework for medical question answering that integrates a shared memory pool with iterative fusion mechanisms. MediHive deploys LLM-based agents that autonomously self-assign specialized roles, conduct initial analyses, detect divergences through conditional evidence-based debates, and locally fuse peer insights over multiple rounds to achieve consensus. Empirically, MediHive outperforms single-LLM and centralized baselines on MedQA and PubMedQA datasets, attaining accuracies of 84.3% and 78.4%, respectively. Our work advances scalable, fault-tolerant D-MAS for medical AI, addressing key limitations of centralized designs while demonstrating superior performance in reasoning-intensive tasks.

</details>


### [151] [daVinci-LLM:Towards the Science of Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27164)
*Yiwei Qin,Yixiu Liu,Tiantian Mi,Muhang Xie,Zhen Huang,Weiye Si,Pengrui Lu,Siyuan Feng,Xia Wu,Liming Liu,Ye Luo,Jinlong Hou,Qipeng Guo,Yu Qiao,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: daVinci-LLM项目填补了预训练研究的空白，结合工业级计算资源与学术研究自由，采用完全开放范式，基于Data Darwinism框架进行系统化数据处理，通过200+控制实验揭示了数据处理深度、领域饱和动态等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练阶段决定模型能力上限，但该领域存在结构性矛盾：拥有计算资源的商业机构因商业压力缺乏透明度，而学术机构虽有研究自由但缺乏预训练规模的计算资源。daVinci-LLM旨在填补这一空白，结合工业级资源与完全研究自由，推动预训练科学的发展。

Method: 采用完全开放范式，将开放性作为科学方法论，发布完整数据处理流水线、训练过程和系统探索结果。使用Data Darwinism框架（L0-L9分类法）进行原则性数据处理。从随机初始化训练30亿参数模型，使用8T tokens，采用两阶段自适应课程学习：从基础能力逐步转向推理密集型增强。通过200+控制实验进行系统探索。

Result: 通过控制实验发现：1）数据处理深度系统性增强能力，成为与规模扩展同等重要的关键维度；2）不同领域呈现不同的饱和动态，需要从比例调整到格式转换的自适应策略；3）组合平衡支持针对性强化同时防止性能崩溃；4）评估协议选择影响对预训练进展的理解。

Conclusion: daVinci-LLM通过结合工业级资源与学术研究自由，填补了预训练研究的空白。采用完全开放范式并基于Data Darwinism框架，揭示了数据处理深度、领域饱和动态等关键发现。通过发布完整探索过程，使社区能够基于这些发现和系统方法论，在预训练领域形成累积性科学知识。

Abstract: The foundational pretraining phase determines a model's capability ceiling, as post-training struggles to overcome capability foundations established during pretraining, yet it remains critically under-explored. This stems from a structural paradox: organizations with computational resources operate under commercial pressures that inhibit transparent disclosure, while academic institutions possess research freedom but lack pretraining-scale computational resources. daVinci-LLM occupies this unexplored intersection, combining industrial-scale resources with full research freedom to advance the science of pretraining. We adopt a fully-open paradigm that treats openness as scientific methodology, releasing complete data processing pipelines, full training processes, and systematic exploration results. Recognizing that the field lacks systematic methodology for data processing, we employ the Data Darwinism framework, a principled L0-L9 taxonomy from filtering to synthesis. We train a 3B-parameter model from random initialization across 8T tokens using a two-stage adaptive curriculum that progressively shifts from foundational capabilities to reasoning-intensive enhancement.
